<span>    <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token attribute"><span class="token class-name">HasPermission</span><span class="token attribute-arguments"><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token string">"Users"</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"Edit"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span></span><span class="token punctuation">]</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token attribute"><span class="token class-name">HttpPost</span></span><span class="token punctuation">]</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token attribute"><span class="token class-name">ValidateAntiForgeryToken</span></span><span class="token punctuation">]</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token keyword">public</span> <span class="token keyword">async</span> <span class="token return-type class-name">Task<span class="token punctuation">&lt;</span>IActionResult<span class="token punctuation">&gt;</span></span> <span class="token function">Edit</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token class-name">UserCreateEditViewModel</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token class-name">CancellationToken</span> ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id <span class="token operator">==</span> <span class="token keyword">null</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token function">BadRequest</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> user <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token keyword">await</span> _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Users</span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Include</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>u <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> u<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UserRoles<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">FirstOrDefaultAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>u <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> u<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id <span class="token operator">==</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Value<span class="token punctuation">,</span> ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span>user <span class="token operator">==</span> <span class="token keyword">null</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token function">NotFound</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token comment">// چک یکتایی نام کاربری (به جز خود کاربر)</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token keyword">await</span> _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Users<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">AnyAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>u <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> u<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Username <span class="token operator">==</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Username <span class="token operator">&amp;&amp;</span> u<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id <span class="token operator">!=</span> user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">,</span> ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            ModelState<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">AddModelError</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token keyword">nameof</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Username<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token string">"این نام کاربری قبلاً استفاده شده است"</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token operator">!</span>ModelState<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsValid<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token keyword">await</span> <span class="token function">PopulateDropdownsAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">,</span> ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token function">View</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Username <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Username<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>FullName <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>FullName<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>PersonnelCode <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>PersonnelCode<span class="token punctuation">?.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>NationalCode <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>NationalCode<span class="token punctuation">?.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Email <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Email<span class="token punctuation">?.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Mobile <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Mobile<span class="token punctuation">?.</span><span class="token function">Trim</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>DepartmentId <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>DepartmentId<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsActive <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsActive<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsLockedOut <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsLockedOut<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>MustChangePassword <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>MustChangePassword<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UpdatedAt <span class="token operator">=</span> DateTime<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UtcNow<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token comment">// اگه رمز جدید داده شده</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">if</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token operator">!</span><span class="token keyword">string</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">IsNullOrWhiteSpace</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Password<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>            user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>PasswordHash <span class="token operator">=</span> _hasher<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Hash</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Password<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token comment">// آپدیت نقش‌ها — اول حذف، بعد اضافه</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> currentRoleIds <span class="token operator">=</span> user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UserRoles<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Select</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ur <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> ur<span class="token punctuation">.</span>RoleId<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">ToList</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> newRoleIds <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>SelectedRoleIds<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token comment">// حذف نقش‌های حذف‌شده</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> toRemove <span class="token operator">=</span> user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UserRoles<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Where</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ur <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> <span class="token operator">!</span>newRoleIds<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Contains</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ur<span class="token punctuation">.</span>RoleId<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">ToList</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UserRoles<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">RemoveRange</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>toRemove<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token comment">// اضافه کردن نقش‌های جدید</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> toAdd <span class="token operator">=</span> newRoleIds<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Where</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>rid <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> <span class="token operator">!</span>currentRoleIds<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Contains</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>rid<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">ToList</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">foreach</span> <span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> rid <span class="token keyword">in</span> toAdd<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>            _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>UserRoles<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Add</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token keyword">new</span> <span class="token constructor-invocation class-name">UserRole</span> <span class="token punctuation">{</span> UserId <span class="token operator">=</span> user<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">,</span> RoleId <span class="token operator">=</span> rid <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">await</span> _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">SaveChangesAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        TempData<span class="token punctuation">[</span><span class="token string">"Success"</span><span class="token punctuation">]</span> <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token string">"کاربر با موفقیت ویرایش شد"</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token keyword">return</span> <span class="token function">RedirectToAction</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token keyword">nameof</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>Index<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>    <span class="token comment">// ============ Helper ============</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token keyword">private</span> <span class="token keyword">async</span> <span class="token return-type class-name">Task</span> <span class="token function">PopulateDropdownsAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token class-name">UserCreateEditViewModel</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">,</span> <span class="token class-name">CancellationToken</span> ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>        <span class="token class-name"><span class="token keyword">var</span></span> roles <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token keyword">await</span> _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Roles</span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Where</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsActive<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">OrderBy</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>DisplayName<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Select</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> <span class="token keyword">new</span> <span class="token constructor-invocation class-name">RoleCheckboxItem</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>                Id <span class="token operator">=</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">,</span></span>
<span>                DisplayName <span class="token operator">=</span> r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>DisplayName<span class="token punctuation">,</span></span>
<span>                IsSelected <span class="token operator">=</span> vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>SelectedRoleIds<span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Contains</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>r<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">ToListAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>AvailableRoles <span class="token operator">=</span> roles<span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span></span>
<span>        vm<span class="token punctuation">.</span>AvailableDepartments <span class="token operator">=</span> <span class="token keyword">await</span> _db<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Departments</span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Where</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>d <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> d<span class="token punctuation">.</span>IsActive<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">OrderBy</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>d <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> d<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Name<span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">Select</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>d <span class="token operator">=&gt;</span> <span class="token keyword">new</span> <span class="token constructor-invocation class-name">DepartmentSelectItem</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">{</span></span>
<span>                Id <span class="token operator">=</span> d<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Id<span class="token punctuation">,</span></span>
<span>                Name <span class="token operator">=</span> d<span class="token punctuation">.</span>Name</span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></span>
<span>            <span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function">ToListAsync</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span>ct<span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token punctuation">;</span></span>
<span>    <span class="token punctuation">}</span></span>

### User

این چک باکس های کنار برای چیه مثلا زده قفل شده یا فلان؟

---

### DeepSeek AI

<p>思考：</p><blockquote><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">The user is asking about the checkboxes / switches shown in the UI. They see:</span></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">In Edit page: "فعال" (Active) is blue/on, "قفل شده" (Locked) is blue/on</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">In Create page: "فعال" is on, "قفل شده" is off, "مجبور به تغییر رمز در ورود بعدی" is off</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">And in Roles section: "مدیر سیستم" with checkbox</span></p></li></ul><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">They're asking what these are for.</span></p><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">I should explain each one:</span></p><ol start="1"><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Checkbox "مدیر سیستم" under نقشها (Roles) - assigning role to user</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Switch "فعال" (Active) - whether account can log in at all</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Switch "قفل شده" (Locked Out) - whether account is temporarily locked</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Switch "مجبور به تغییر رمز در ورود بعدی" (Must Change Password on next login) - force password change</span></p></li></ol><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">But wait - there's an issue in the Edit screenshot: "قفل شده" is turned ON (blue) for admin. This means admin is currently locked out! But they can still login because... actually no, let me check the logic. In AuthService.LoginAsync, we check:</span></p><div class="md-code-block md-code-block-dark"><div class="md-code-block-banner-wrap"><div class="md-code-block-banner md-code-block-banner-lite"><div d="M-5.24537e-07 0C-2.34843e-07 6.62742 5.37258 12 12 12L0 12L-5.24537e-07 0Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></div><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">So IsLockedOut=true but LockoutEnd=null means it doesn't actually block. But it's confusing. We should clarify.</span></p><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Also "فعال" is ON (blue) - meaning active. Good.</span></p><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Let me explain each control clearly. I should also point out this issue with LockedOut.</span></p><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">Actually looking more carefully at the Edit screenshot - "قفل شده" appears to be blue (toggled ON). This is a bit concerning but since LockoutEnd is null, admin can still login. Let me note this.</span></p><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">I should be brief and clear about each control's purpose.</span></p></blockquote><br/><h1><span class="">📖 توضیح کنترل‌های فرم</span></h1><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">توی فرم کاربر، ۴ تا کنترل داری که بذار یک‌به‌یک توضیح بدم:</span></p><hr><h2><span class="">۱️⃣ چک‌باکس «مدیر سیستم» (زیر نقش‌ها)</span></h2><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">این </span><strong><span class="">نقش‌ها (Roles)</span></strong><span class=""> هستن. یعنی این کاربر عضو کدوم نقش‌هاست.</span></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">اگه تیک بزنی → کاربر عضو اون نقش میشه → همه‌ی دسترسی‌های اون نقش رو به ارث می‌بره</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">بعداً می‌تونیم نقش‌های بیشتری بسازیم (مثل «اپراتور تولید»، «انباردار»، «حسابدار») و اینجا نمایش داده میشن</span></p></li></ul><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">الان فقط یه نقش داری:</span></strong><span class=""> </span>`مدیر سیستم`<span class=""> (Administrator). بعداً که نقش‌ها رو ساختیم، این‌جا پر میشه.</span></p><hr><h2><span class="">۲️⃣ کلید «فعال» (IsActive)</span></h2><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">معنی:</span></strong><span class=""> آیا این حساب کاربری </span><strong><span class="">کلاً</span></strong><span class=""> قابل استفاده‌ست؟</span></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">✅ </span><strong><span class="">روشن</span></strong><span class=""> → کاربر می‌تونه لاگین کنه</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">❌ </span><strong><span class="">خاموش</span></strong><span class=""> → کاربر اصلاً نمی‌تونه وارد سیستم بشه (حتی با رمز درست)</span></p></li></ul><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">کِی خاموشش کنی؟</span></strong></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">کاربر از شرکت خارج شده (منتظر پاک کردن کامل)</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">کاربر مرخصی طولانی مدت رفته</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">کاربر جدید که هنوز آماده نیست</span></p></li></ul><hr><h2><span class="">۳️⃣ کلید «قفل شده» (IsLockedOut)</span></h2><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">معنی:</span></strong><span class=""> آیا حساب </span><strong><span class="">موقتاً</span></strong><span class=""> به دلیل تلاش‌های ناموفق قفل شده؟</span></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">این کلید </span><strong><span class="">خودکار</span></strong><span class=""> توسط سیستم پر میشه: بعد از ۵ بار رمز اشتباه، سیستم خودش قفلش می‌کنه برای ۱۵ دقیقه</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">تو معمولاً دستی روش نمی‌کنی</span></strong><span class=""> — مدیر سیستم می‌تونه اگه کاربر قفل شد، دستی </span><strong><span class="">خاموشش</span></strong><span class=""> کنه تا کاربر فوراً بتونه دوباره لاگین کنه (بدون انتظار ۱۵ دقیقه)</span></p></li></ul><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">⚠️ توی اسکرین‌شات تو، این کلید برای admin روشنه!</span></strong><span class=""> این طبیعیه چون موقع ساخت admin توی Seeder، </span>`IsLockedOut = false`<span class=""> گذاشتیم. اگه الان روشنه، یعنی احتمالاً وقتی داشتی تست می‌کردی رمز اشتباه زدی. </span><strong><span class="">خاموشش کن.</span></strong></p><blockquote><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">نکته فنی:</span></strong><span class=""> منطق قفل شدن هم توی </span>`IsLockedOut`<span class=""> هست و هم توی </span>`LockoutEnd`<span class="">. یعنی فقط </span>`IsLockedOut = true`<span class=""> کافی نیست، باید </span>`LockoutEnd`<span class=""> هم توی آینده باشه. الان چون </span>`LockoutEnd = null`<span class=""> هست، admin هنوز می‌تونه لاگین کنه.</span></p></blockquote><hr><h2><span class="">۴️⃣ کلید «مجبور به تغییر رمز در ورود بعدی» (MustChangePassword)</span></h2><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">معنی:</span></strong><span class=""> آیا کاربر بعد از لاگین </span><strong><span class="">باید</span></strong><span class=""> رمزش رو عوض کنه؟</span></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">✅ </span><strong><span class="">روشن</span></strong><span class=""> → بعد از لاگین موفق، کاربر خودکار می‌ره به صفحه‌ی تغییر رمز، و تا رمز رو عوض نکنه به جای دیگه دسترسی نداره</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">❌ </span><strong><span class="">خاموش</span></strong><span class=""> → کاربر می‌تونه از همون اول همه جا بره</span></p></li></ul><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">کِی روشنش کنی؟</span></strong></p><ul><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">وقتی مدیر سیستم برای کاربر جدید رمز موقت می‌ذاره (مثلاً </span>`Welcome@123`<span class="">)</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">وقتی رمزی لورفته و می‌خوای کاربر سریع عوضش کنه</span></p></li><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">برای اولین ورود اجباری</span></p></li></ul><hr><h1><span class="">🎯 خلاصه در یه جدول:</span></h1><div class="ds-scroll-area نزن</span></td></tr></tbody></table></div><hr><h1><span class="">⚠️ یه نکته مهم</span></h1><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><span class="">توی اسکرین‌شات اول (Edit admin)، </span><strong><span class="">کلید «قفل شده» روشنه (آبی)</span></strong><span class="">. این یعنی یه بار رمز اشتباه زدی و </span>`AccessFailedCount`<span class=""> به ۵ رسیده. این رو </span><strong><span class="">خاموشش کن</span></strong><span class=""> و ذخیره کن تا admin تمیز بشه.</span></p><hr><h1><span class="">🛑 الان تست کن:</span></h1><ol start="1"><li><p class="ds-markdown-paragraph"><strong><span class="">قفل شده</span></strong><span class=""> رو برای admin </span><strong><span class="">خاموش</span></strong><span class=""> کن → </span><strong><span class="">ذخیره</span></strong></p></li><li><p
